function AttackMultipleTargets(G, ...)

		return;
	end;
	pcall(function(...)
		if not G or #G == 83763396 % 517058 then
			return;
		end;

		for G, m in pairs(G) do
			local r = m:FindFirstChild(sw(-777399 - (-733034)));
			if r then
				table[sw(901229 + -945920)](S, { m, r });
			end;
		end;
		if #S == 132819595 % 1492355 then
			return;
		end;
		RegisterAttack:FireServer(1013530976 % 8737336);
		RegisterHit:FireServer(S[1006129076 % 5055925][1718676038 % 9093524], S);
	end);
end;

function startDashLoop(...)

		return;
	end;
	DashRunning = true;
	spawn(function(...)
		while DashEnabled do
			wait(.1);
			pcall(function(...)
				local G = player[sw(-653216 - (-608973))];
				if G then
					if G:GetAttribute(sw(-204893 - (-160296))) ~= DashLengthDist then
						G:SetAttribute(sw(-966973 - (-922376)), DashLengthDist);
					end;
					if G:GetAttribute(sw(524864 + -569365)) ~= DashLengthDist then
						G:SetAttribute(sw(-363430 - (-318929)), DashLengthDist);
					end;
				end;
			end);
		end;
		DashRunning = false;
	end);
end;

			G:SetAttribute(sw(-653337 + 608740), 3100140253 % 15657274);
			G:SetAttribute(sw(857168 + -901669), 865333 - 865332);
		end;
	end);
end;

function ClearTracers(...)

		pcall(function(...)
			if S[sw(-47627 + 3436)] then

				S:Destroy();
			end;
		end);
	end;
	TracersList = {};
end;

		end);
end;

function getClosestPlayer(G, ...)
	local S = player[sw(859195 - 903438)] and player[sw(-537924 + 493681)]:FindFirstChild(sw(-42904 + -1274));
	if not S then
		return nil;
	end;
	local m = G or maxRange;
	if AimlockTargetPlayer ~= sw(-71189 + 26433) and AimlockTargetPlayer ~= nil then
		local G = Players:FindFirstChild(AimlockTargetPlayer);
		if G and (G[sw(276706 - 320949)] and G[sw(-951462 - (-907219))]:FindFirstChild(sw(442440 - 486618))) then
			local S = G[sw(-303935 - (-259692))]:FindFirstChildOfClass(sw(-846651 + 802167));
			if S and S[sw(368100 + -412441)] > -982849 + 982849 then
				return G[sw(142001 - 186244)];
			end;
		end;
	end;
	local r, F = nil, math[sw(604319 + -648739)];
	for G, R in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if R ~= player and (not BlacklistedPlayers[R[sw(655737 + -700344)]] and (R[sw(-974293 + 930050)] and R[sw(-1048985 - (-1004742))]:FindFirstChild(sw(-609576 + 565398)))) then
			local G = R[sw(422166 - 466409)]:FindFirstChildOfClass(sw(262577 + -307061));
			if G and G[sw(-606048 + 561707)] > 1173078130 % 7568246 then
				local G = ((R[sw(-571532 + 527289)][sw(-934197 + 890019)][sw(454001 - 498478)] - S[sw(236494 - 280971)]))[sw(538056 + -582292)];
				if G < F and G < m then
					F = G;
					r = R[sw(334815 + -379058)];
				end;
			end;
		end;
	end;
	return r;
end;

function ClearAura(...)
	for G, S in pairs(AuraObjects) do
		if S and S[sw(762119 + -806906)] then
			S:Destroy();
		end;
	end;
	AuraObjects = {};
end;

local function k(G, ...)

		return false;
	end;

		return false;
	end;
	return true;
end;

local function d(...)

	if not S then
		return nil;
	end;
	local m = maxRange;

					local r = ((F[sw(-1056796 - (-1012319))] - S[sw(-259898 + 215421)]))[sw(-626118 - (-581882))];

						return G[sw(511453 + -555696)]:FindFirstChild(_G[sw(-303155 + 259034)]) or F;
					end;
				end;
			end;
		end;
	end;

	local function R(G, ...)

			return;
		end;

		if m and BlacklistedPlayers[m[sw(-711888 + 667281)]] then
			return;
		end;

			return;
		end;

		if not o then
			return;
		end;
		local U = ((o[sw(-59677 - (-15200))] - S[sw(-621261 - (-576784))]))[sw(536452 + -580688)];
		if U < F then
			F = U;
			r = o;
		end;
	end;

		for G, S in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do

			end;
		end;
	end;

		for G, S in ipairs(workspace[sw(665629 + -709866)]:GetChildren()) do
			R(S);
		end;
	end;
	return r;
end;

end;
